Principal Risks and Uncertainties                                                       主要風險及不明確因素
The Group is exposed to various risks and uncertainties which are specific to           本集團面臨多項本集團及╱或本集團經營所在
the Group and/or the industries in which the Group operates. These risks may            行業特有的風險及不明確因素。此等風險可能
materially affect the Group’s business operations, financial condition, results         會重大影響本集團的業務營運、財務狀況、營
of operations and business prospects. The Group has identified the key risks            運業績及業務前景。本集團已識別出以下的主
and uncertainties as follows:                                                           要風險及不明確因素：

Operating at close to full capacity                                                     營運臨近產能上限
The Group is operating at close to full capacity in its existing glass epoxy            本集團現有的環氧玻璃纖維覆銅面板生產廠房
laminates production plants. Accordingly, the Group’s ability to grow its               已臨近產能上限。因此，本集團的業務增長能
business is dependent upon it acquiring additional production capacity                  力取決於能否通過擴充現有廠房或通過興建或
through either the expansion of its existing plants or the building or acquisition      收購額外廠房以獲得額外產能。概無保證本集
of additional plants. There can be no assurance that the Group will be able             團能夠在近期達至計劃中的產能增長，或可能
to achieve its planned increases in production capacity in the near future or           根本無法達致產能增長。倘本集團無法達至產
at all. Failure to achieve this expansion in production capacity would limit the        能增長，這將限制本集團保持競爭力及爭取商
Group’s ability to remain competitive and to maximise business opportunities            機的能力，從而或會對本集團的財務狀況、營
which may in turn have a material adverse effect on the financial condition,            運業績及業務前景產生重大不利影響。
results of operations and business prospects of the Group.

on 12 August 2024, GRFP were not able to pay the bond interests due                   二四年八月十二日的公告，由於其惡劣財務狀
to its adverse financial condition. Also, according to the announcement of            況，廣州富力地產故此未能支付其債券利息。
GRFP dated 19 December 2024, GRFP has announced its debt restructing                  另外，根據廣州富力地產日期為二零二四年
plan for the above GRFP debt instruments. The Group had performed an                  十二月十九日的公告，廣州富力地產公佈了上
impairment assessment on the debt instruments held under the expected                 述債務工具的債務重組方案。本集團已根據預
credit loss (“ECL”) model. The measurement of ECL is a function of the                期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模型對所持有之
probability of default and loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if      債務工具進行減值評估。預期信貸虧損之計量
there is a default), with the assessment of the probability of default and loss       是違約概率及違約損失率（即發生違約時的損失
given default being based on historical data and forward-looking information.         程度）的函數，而違約概率及違約損失率乃基於
The estimation of lifetime ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-weighted          歷史數據及前瞻性資料評估。全期預期信貸虧
amount that is determined with the respective risks of default occurring as the       損的估計反映無偏頗及概率加權金額，該金額
weights, and also with reference to the time value of money. In determining           以發生違約的相應風險為權重釐定，同時亦參
lifetime ECL on the Group’s debt instruments for the year, the management             考貨幣的時間價值。於釐定本集團於年內之債
had taken into accounts factors including the downgrading of credit rating of         務工具的全期預期信貸虧損時，管理層考慮各
the debt instruments by the credit rating agencies, the defaults of the bond          種因素，包括信貸評級機構下調債務工具的信
issuer in making payments of interest and principal for its indebtedness, and         貸評級、債券發行人拖欠其債務的利息及本金
forward-looking information including the future macroeconomic conditions at          付款以及前瞻性資料，包括債券發行人現時經
places where the bond issuer is operating.                                            營所在地的未來宏觀經濟狀況。

The Group recognised impairment loss under ECL model of HK$82,934,000                 經參考違約風險、回收率及前瞻性資料之調
in profit or loss for the reporting period and the accumulated ECL of                 整後，本集團根據預期信貸虧損模式就以上

     Division and responsibilities                                                  分工及職責
     The Board is headed by the Chairman whose role differs from that of            董事會由主席領導，其職務有別於本公
     the Managing Director of the Company. The roles with the Chairman              司董事總經理。主席及董事總經理各有
     and Managing Director are segregated and are not exercised by the              獨立職務，且非由同一名人士擔任。
     same individual.

     The duties of the Chairman include (but not limited to) the following:         主席職責包括但不限於：

     •       scheduling meetings that enable the Board to perform                   •   安排會議以便董事會履行職責，
             its duties and responsibilities and to ensure all key and                  並確保董事會及時討論所有重要
             appropriate issues are discussed by the Board in a timely                  及適當事宜，而不會干擾本公司
             manner without interfering the Company’s operations;                       之營運；

     •       preparing meeting agendas after consultations with the                 •   經與董事總經理磋商後編製會議
             Managing Director;                                                         議程；

     •       exercising control over quality, quantity and timeliness of the        •   監控管理層與董事會之間的溝通
             flow of information between management and the Board and                   的質量、數量和及時性，確保董
             to ensure decisions are made on a fully informed basis by the              事於完全知情情況下作出決定；
             Directors; and                                                             及

     •       assisting in ensuring compliance with the Listing Rules and            •   協助確保遵從上市規則及本公司
             the Company’s guidelines on corporate governance.                          之企業管治指引。

     The Managing Director is mainly responsible for the overall strategic          董事總經理主要負責整體策略規劃及本
     planning and day-to-day management of the Group.                               集團日常管理。

RESPONSIBILITIES OF DIRECTORS AND                                                  董事及治理層就綜合財務報
THOSE CHARGED WITH GOVERNANCE                                                      表須承擔的責任
FOR THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL
STATEMENTS
The directors of the Company are responsible for the preparation of the            貴公司董事須負責根據香港會計師公會頒佈的
consolidated financial statements that give a true and fair view in accordance     香港財務申報準則及香港公司條例的披露要求
with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA and the disclosure requirements of                擬備真實而中肯的綜合財務報表，並對其認為
the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance, and for such internal control as the            為使綜合財務報表的擬備不存在由於欺詐或錯
directors determine is necessary to enable the preparation of consolidated         誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述所需的內部控制負責。
financial statements that are free from material misstatement, whether due to
fraud or error.

In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors are              在擬備綜合財務報表時，董事負責評估 貴集
responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern,      團持續經營的能力，並在適當情況下披露與持
disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going concern and using the          續經營有關的事項，以及使用持續經營為會計
going concern basis of accounting unless the directors either intend to            基礎，除非董事有意將 貴集團清盤或停止經
liquidate the Group or to cease operations, or have no realistic alternative but   營，或別無其他實際的替代方案。
to do so.

Those charged with governance are responsible for overseeing the Group’s           治理層須負責監督   貴集團的財務報告過程。
financial reporting process.

they could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of              期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表

1.    GENERAL                                                                         1.   一般事項
      Kingboard Laminates Holdings Limited (the “Company”) was                             建滔積層板控股有限公司（「本公司」）在
      incorporated and registered as an exempted company with limited                      開曼群島註冊成立並登記為一家獲豁免
      liability in the Cayman Islands and its shares are listed on the Main                之有限公司，其股份於香港聯合交易所
      Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                         有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。本公司
      Exchange”). Its immediate parent company is Jamplan (BVI) Limited,                   之直屬母公司為Jamplan (BVI) Limited，
      a limited liability company incorporated in the British Virgin Islands and           該公司為一家於英屬處女群島註冊成立
      in the opinion of the directors of the Company (the “Directors”), its                之有限公司，而本公司董事（「董事」）認
      ultimate holding company is Kingboard Holdings Limited (“KHL”), an                   為，本公司之最終控股公司為建滔集團
      exempted limited liability company incorporated in the Cayman Islands                有限公司（「建滔集團」），該公司為一家
      with its shares also listed on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange.                 於開曼群島註冊成立並獲豁免之有限公
      The addresses of the registered office and principal place of business               司，其股份於聯交所主板上市。本公司
      of the Company are disclosed in the section headed “Corporate                        註冊辦事處之地址及主要營業地點於本
      Information” of the annual report.                                                   年報「公司資料」一節中披露。

      The Company is an investment holding company and the principal                       本公司為投資控股公司，其主要附屬公
      activities of its principal subsidiaries are set out in Note 40.                     司之主要業務載於附註40。

      The consolidated financial statements are presented in Hong Kong                     綜合財務報表以港元（「港元」）呈列，港
      dollars (“HK$”), which is different from the Company’s functional                    元有別於本公司的功能貨幣人民幣（「人
      currency of Renminbi (“RMB”), the currency of the primary economic                   民幣」），即本公司經營所在的主要經濟
      environment in which the Company operates. Because the                               環境的貨幣。由於本公司股份於聯交所
      Company’s shares are listed on the Stock Exchange, where most                        上市，其大部分投資者均位於香港，因
      of its investors are located in Hong Kong, therefore the Directors                   此董事認為以港元呈列綜合財務報表更
      considered it is more relevant convenient for the users of the                       方便綜合財務報表的使用者。
      consolidated financial statements to present the consolidated financial
      statement in HK$.

     The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in the                         本集團於本年度首次應用該等修訂本。
     current year.

     The 2020 Amendments provide clarification and additional guidance                     二零二零年修訂本為評估報告日期起延
     on the assessment of right to defer settlement for at least twelve                    遲最少十二個月之結算分類為流動或非
     months from reporting date for classification of liabilities as current or            流動負債提供澄清及額外指引，當中：
     non-current, which:

     •       specify that the classification of liabilities as current or non-             •    訂明負債應基於報告期末存在之
             current should be based on rights that are in existence at the                     權利而分類為流動或非流動。具
             end of the reporting period. Specifically, the amendments                          體而言，修訂本澄清該分類不應
             clarify that the classification should not be affected by                          受到管理層在十二個月內清償負
             management intentions or expectations to settle the liability                      債的意圖或期望所影響。
             within 12 months.

     •       clarify that the settlement of a liability can be a transfer of               •    澄清可透過現金轉賬、轉移貨物
             cash, goods or services, or the entity’s own equity instruments                    或服務或實體本身的權益工具轉
             to the counterparty. If a liability has terms that could, at the                   讓予對手方清償負債。倘負債具
             option of the counterparty, result in its settlement by the                        有條款，可由對手方選擇透過轉
             transfer of the entity’s own equity instruments, these terms do                    讓實體本身之權益工具進行結
             not affect its classification as current or non-current only if the                算，則僅當實體應用香港會計準
             entity recognises the option separately as an equity instrument                    則第32號「金融工具：呈列」將
             applying HKAS 32 “Financial Instruments: Presentation”.                            期權分開確認為權益工具時，該

            differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised                     項負債一般會就所有應課稅臨時

            probable that taxable profits will be available against which                 在可能出現可利用臨時差額扣稅

            temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other               易初步確認資產及負債且不影響

            foreseeable future.                                                                   見將來撥回之情況為限。

            the end of the reporting period.                                                      量。

            is always presumed to be recovered entirely through sale.                             常被認為通過出售方能完全收回
                                                                                                  的永久產權土地除外。

             Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                           全期預期信貸虧損指將因相關工

             relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”)                      件而導致的預期信貸虧損。12
             represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to                      個月預期信貸虧損（「12個月預
             result from default events that are possible within 12 months                   期信貸虧損」）指預計因報告日期
             after the reporting date. Assessments are done based on the                     後12個月內可能出現的違約事
             Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted for factors                 件導致的全期預期信貸虧損的一
             that are specific to the debtors, general economic conditions                   部分。評估基於本集團的過往信

                    ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-weighted                               和前瞻性信息。預期信

                    of default occurring as the weights. The Group has                              生相關違約的風險為權

      Deferred taxation on investment properties (Notes 14 and 19)                        投資物業之遞延稅項（附註14及19）
      For the purposes of measuring deferred tax liabilities or deferred tax              就計量利用公平值模式的投資物業所產
      assets arising from investment properties that are measured using the               生的遞延稅項負債或遞延稅項資產而
      fair value model, the Directors have reviewed the Group’s investment                言，董事已檢討本集團於二零二四年
      property portfolios as at 31 December 2024, and concluded that                      十二月三十一日的投資物業組合，並
      the Group’s investment properties located in the PRC with carrying                  認為本集團賬面值為228,017,000港元
      amounts of HK$228,017,000 (2023: HK$228,601,000) are held under                     （二零二三年：228,601,000港元）位於
      a business model which objective is to consume substantially all of the             中國的投資物業乃按業務模式持有該等
      economic benefits embodied in the investment properties over time,                  物業，其目的是隨時間耗用投資物業所
      rather than through sale. Therefore, the Directors have determined the              包含的絕大部分經濟利益（而非透過出
      presumption that the carrying amounts of such investment properties                 售）。故董事決定駁回有關投資物業的
      are recovered entirely through sale is rebutted. As a result, the Group             賬面值可全數透過出售而收回的假定。
      has not recognised deferred taxes on LAT in respect of changes in                   因此，本集團並無就有關投資物業的公
      fair value of such investment properties.                                           平值變動確認土地增值稅的遞延稅項。

      As at 31 December 2024, the investment properties located in                        於二零二四年十二月三十一日，賬面值
      Hong Kong and the United Kingdom with carrying amounts of                           分別為355,750,000港元（二零二三年：

      (2023: HK$659,022,000) respectively are not held under a business                   （二零二三年：659,022,000港元）之位
      model with the objective of which is to recover the economic benefits               於香港及英國之投資物業，並非根據旨
      of the investment properties entirely through use. No deferred tax is               在透過使用收回全部投資物業經濟利益

      where inputs can be observable, other than quoted prices in the                      第二級可觀察輸入數據。如無第二級輸

9.    INCOME TAX EXPENSE (continued)                                                  9.   所得稅開支（續）
      Under the two-tiered profits tax rates regime of Hong Kong Profits                   根據利得稅兩級制或香港利得稅制度，
      Tax, the first HK$2 million of profits of the qualifying group entity will           合資格集團實體的首二百萬港元利潤將
      be taxed at 8.25%, and profits above HK$2 million will be taxed at                   按8.25%徵稅，而超過二百萬港元的利
      16.5%. The profits of group entities not qualifying for the two-tiered               潤則須按16.5%徵稅。不符合利得稅兩
      profits tax rates regime will continue to be taxed at a flat rate of                 級制的集團實體的利潤將繼續按16.5%
      16.5%. Accordingly, the Hong Kong Profits Tax of the qualifying group                的固定稅率徵稅。因此，合資格集團
      entity is calculated at 8.25% on the first HK$2 million of the estimated             實體首二百萬港元估計應課稅溢利將
      assessable profits and at 16.5% on the estimated assessable profits                  按 8.25% 的 稅 率 繳 納 香 港 利 得 稅 ， 而
      above HK$2 million.                                                                  二百萬港元以上之估計應課稅溢利則按
                                                                                           16.5%的稅率繳稅。

      The Group is operating in the UK where the Pillar Two Rules are                      本集團於英國經營業務，而支柱二規則
      effective. The Group’s annual effective tax rates of the Group’s                     於英國生效。本集團在英國經營業務的
      operation in the UK is higher than 15%. After taking into account the                實際年稅率高於15%。在考慮根據管理
      adjustments under the Pillar Two Rules based on management’s best                    層的最佳估計而作出的支柱二規則下的
      estimate, the management of the Group considered the Group is not                    調整後，本集團管理層認為本集團毋須
      liable to top-up tax under the Pillar Two Rules.                                     根據支柱二規則繳納補足稅款。

      The Group is also operating in Thailand where the Pillar Two Rules                   本集團亦於泰國經營業務，而支柱二規
      are enacted but not effective and will come into effect from 1 January               則已於泰國頒佈但尚未生效，並將自二
      2025. Since the Pillar Two Rules were not yet effective at the reporting             零二五年一月一日起生效。由於支柱二
      date, the Group has no related current tax exposure. The Group has                   規則於報告日期尚未生效，故本集團並
      applied the temporary mandatory exception from recognising and                       無相關即期稅項風險。本集團已針對補
      disclosing deferred tax assets and liabilities for the impacts of the top-           足稅的影響應用有關確認及披露的遞延
      up tax and accounts for it as a current tax when it is incurred.                     稅項資產及負債的臨時強制豁免，並在
                                                                                           產生時將其入賬列作即期稅項。

      The effective tax rates are lower than 15% and might be subject to                   實際稅率低於15%，可能須繳納支柱二
      Pillar Two income taxes. However, this information is based on the                   所得稅。然而，此資料乃基於本集團對
      Group’s best estimate of the calculation of globe income under the                   根據支柱二規則所計算全球收入的最佳
      Pillar Two Rules. Because not all of the adjustments that would have                 估計得出。由於並非所有法例要求的調
      been required by the legislation were made, the actual impact that the               整均已作出，因此倘支柱二規則生效，
      Pillar Two Rules would have had on the Group’s results if it had been                其對本集團業績的實際影響可能會有重
      in effect may have been significantly different. The Group is continuing             大差異。本集團將繼續評估支柱二所得
      to assess the impact of the Pillar Two income taxes legislation on its               稅法例對其未來財務表現的影響。
      future financial performance.

      Taxation arising in other jurisdictions is calculated at the rates                   其他司法權區之稅項乃按有關司法權區
      prevailing in the relevant jurisdictions.                                            之適用稅率計算。

14. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES                                                          14. 投資物業
                                                                                                            HK$’000
                                                                                                             千港元


       FAIR VALUE                                                    公平值
       At 1 January 2023                                             於二零二三年一月一日                            1,268,425
       Loss on fair value changes                                    公平值變動虧損                                  (34,867)
       Exchange adjustments                                          匯兌調整                                      32,755


       At 31 December 2023                                           於二零二三年十二月三十一日                         1,266,313
       Loss on fair value changes                                    公平值變動虧損                                 (22,940)
       Exchange adjustments                                          匯兌調整                                    (14,238)


       At 31 December 2024                                           於二零二四年十二月三十一日                         1,229,135


      The Group leases out various offices under operating leases with                  本集團根據經營租賃出租多個辦公室，
      rentals payable monthly. The leases typically run for a fixed period of           每月收取租金。租賃一般附有介乎1至7
      1 to 7 years (2023: 1 to 7 years).                                                年的固定租期（二零二三年：1至7年）。

      The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of the              本集團不會因租賃安排而承擔外匯風
      lease arrangement, as all leases are denominated in the respective                險，因為所有租賃均以集團實體各自的
      functional currencies of group entities. The lease contracts do not               功能貨幣列值。租約並不包括餘值擔保
      contain residual value guarantee and/or lessee’s option to purchase               及╱或承租人於租期末購買物業的選擇
      the property at the end of lease term.                                            權。

      In determining the fair value of the relevant properties, the                     為釐定相關物業的公平值，管理層就公
      management determines the appropriate valuation techniques and                    平值計量釐定適當的估值技術及輸入數
      inputs for fair value measurements.                                               據。

      The Group engages an independent qualified valuer to perform its                  本集團委聘獨立合資格估值師估值。管
      valuations. The management works closely with the independent                     理層與獨立合資格估值師緊密合作，
      qualified valuer to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and            共同制定模型之適當估值技術及輸入數
      inputs to the model. The persons in charge of financial matters report            據。財務事宜負責人每季向董事匯報管
      the management findings to the Directors every quarter to explain the             理層之發現，闡明資產及負債公平值波
      cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets and liabilities.            動之因由。

      The Group’s investment properties were classified as Level 3 of the               本集團投資物業於二零二四年及二零
      fair value hierarchy as at 31 December 2024 and 2023.                             二三年十二月三十一日分類為第三級公
                                                                                        平值架構級別。

      2024 and 2023 had been arrived at on the basis of a valuation carried             二三年十二月三十一日之公平值乃按與

      qualified valuer not connected to the Group. Ravia Global Appraisal               豐環球評估諮詢有限公司進行估值之基
      Advisory Limited is a member of the Hong Kong Institute of Surveyors.             準達致。瑞豐環球評估諮詢有限公司為
      The valuation was determined based on direct comparison method                    香港測量師學會會員。估值乃按直接比

23. PROPERTIES HELD FOR                                                        23. 待發展物業（續）
    DEVELOPMENT (continued)
      In the opinion of the Directors, the properties held for development       董事認為待發展物業分類為流動資產，
      were classified as current assets as the properties were held for sale     乃由於在本集團的正常營運週期，持有

30. SHARE OPTIONS                                                                    30. 購股權
      The share option scheme of the Company (“KLHL Scheme”) was                          本公司的購股權計劃（「建滔積層板計
      approved by the Shareholders and the shareholders of KHL on 29                      劃」）已於二零一七年五月二十九日獲股
      May 2017, and the KLHL Scheme took effect upon obtaining approval                   東及建滔集團股東批准，而建滔積層板
      from the Listing Committee of the Stock Exchange on 2 November                      計劃於獲得聯交所上市委員會批准後於
      2017. The purpose of the KLHL Scheme is to provide incentive or                     二零一七年十一月二日生效。建滔積層

31. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                        31. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will           資本風險管理本集團管理其資本以確保
      be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to             通過優化債務與股本結存為權益持有人
      stakeholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance.             爭取最高回報，使本集團旗下實體能夠
      The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.                   持續經營。本集團之整體策略與過往年
                                                                                        度相同。

      The capital structure of the Group consists of bank borrowings                    誠如綜合權益變動表所披露，本集團之
      and lease liabilities, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity                資本結構包括銀行借貸及租賃負債（扣
      attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued share                    除現金及現金等值項目）及本公司持有
      capital, share premium, reserves and retained profits as disclosed in             人應佔權益（包括已發行股本、股份溢
      the consolidated statement of changes in equity.                                  價、儲備及保留溢利）。

      The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.                董事每半年審閱資本結構。作為審閱一
      As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital and            部分，董事將考慮資本成本，及與各類
      the risks associated with each class of capital. In the opinion of the            別資本相關之風險。董事認為，本集團
      Directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure through           將通過支付股息、發行新股、購回股
      the payment of dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as                 份、發行新債或者贖回現有債項，以平
      well as the issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.                 衡整體資本結構。

            Credit risk and impairment assessment                                     信貸風險及減值評估
            Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties            信貸風險指本集團交易對手因違
            default on their contractual obligations resulting in financial           反其合約義務而導致本集團產生
            losses to the Group. The Group’s maximum exposure                         財務虧損的風險。倘交易方於報
            to credit risk in the event of the counterparties’ failure to             告期末未能履行彼等就各類已確
            perform their obligations at the end of the reporting period              認金融資產之承擔，則本集團須
            in relation to each class of recognised financial assets is the           承受之最高信貸風險為於綜合財
            carrying amount of those assets as stated in the consolidated             務狀況表所載該等資產賬面值。
            statement of financial position.

            Trade receivables arising from contracts with customers                   由客戶合約產生的貿易應收賬款
            In order to minimise the credit risk, the Directors have                  為將信貸風險降至最低，董事已
            delegated the management to be responsible for determination              委派管理層負責制訂信貸限額、
            of credit limits, credit approvals for determination of credit            就制訂信貸限額給予的信貸審
            limits, credit approvals and other monitoring procedures                  批、信貸審批及其他監控程序，
            to ensure that follow-up action is taken to recover overdue               以確保採取跟進措施收回逾期未
            debts. In this regard, the Directors consider that the Group’s            付之債項。就此而言，本公司董
            credit risk is significantly reduced.                                     事認為，本集團之信貸風險已大
                                                                                      幅降低。

            The Group has no significant concentration of credit risk                 本集團並無高度集中之貿易應收
            on trade receivables with exposure spread over a number                   賬款信貸風險，風險由多個交易
            of counterparties and customers. However, the Group is                    方及客戶分攤。然而，本集團的
            exposed to the concentration on geographic segment in the                 風險集中於中國之地區分部。於
            PRC. At 31 December 2024, approximately 92% (2023: 90%)                   二零二四年十二月三十一日，本
            of the Group’s trade receivables are arising from the PRC.                集團約92%（二零二三年：90%）
                                                                                      之貿易應收賬款源自中國。

            Except for credit-impaired trade receivables which are                    除個別進行減值評估的信貸減值
            assessed for impairment individually, the remaining trade                 貿易應收賬款外，其餘貿易應收
            receivables are grouped based on shared credit risk                       賬款參照本集團內部信貸評級，

36. CONTINGENT LIABILITIES                                                                          36. 或然負債
      On 3 August 2011, Annuity & Re Life Ltd (the “Petitioner”), the non-                               於二零一一年八月三日，建滔銅箔集
      controlling shareholder of Kingboard Copper Foil Holdings Limited                                  團有限公司（「建滔銅箔」）非控股股東
      (“KBCF”), presented a petition in the Supreme Court of Bermuda                                                         （「呈請人」）根據一
                                                                                                         Annuity & Re Life Ltd
      (the “Petition”) in respect of KBCF against its controlling shareholders                           項指建滔銅箔事務已經或現正進行的方
      based on a complaint that the affairs of KBCF had been and/or                                      式乃壓榨或不合理地不利於呈請人的投
      were being conducted in a manner which was oppressive or unfairly                                  訴，針對建滔銅箔控股股東向百慕達高
      prejudicial to the Petitioner.                                                                     級法院遞交呈請書（「呈請」）。

      The controlling shareholders of KBCF were eventually successful in                                 建滔銅箔控股股東最終於二零一七年三
      defending the case following a favourable judgement by the Bermuda                                 月二十四日獲百慕達上訴法院頒下有利
      Court of Appeal dated 24 March 2017. Subsequently, the Petitioner                                  裁決而成功就該案抗辯。呈請人其後向
      filed an appeal with the Privy Council. Such appeal was withdrawn                                  樞密院提出上訴。經呈請人與呈請的答
      by the Petitioner following a settlement agreement reached by the                                  辯人達成和解協議後，呈請人撤回有關
      Petitioner and the respondents in the Petition.                                                    上訴。

      D u r i n g t h e y e a r e n d e d 31 D e c e m b e r 2019, t h e c o n t r o l l i n g           截至二零一九年十二月三十一日止年
      shareholders of KBCF had purchased all of the remaining issued and                                 度，建滔銅箔控股股東已收購建滔銅箔
      outstanding ordinary shares in the capital of KBCF and KBCF has                                    股本中所有餘下已發行及發行在外的普
      become a wholly-owned subsidiary of the Group.                                                     通股，而建滔銅箔成為本集團全資附屬
                                                                                                         公司。

      On 22 October 2018, the Petitioner commenced an action in the                                      於二零一八年十月二十二日，呈請人根
      Supreme Court of Bermuda under Civil Jurisdiction 2018: No. 359                                    據民事司法管轄範圍2018：第359號針
      against the respondents in the Petition based on purported breach of                               對呈請的答辯人向百慕達高等法院提
      the settlement agreement (the “Settlement Action”).                                                出訴訟，表示違反和解協議（「和解訴
